Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the electricity object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 971. The moduli space has dimension at most 10 with 1 connected components.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 971 + O(1/n^10). Summing over all modes and applying the electricity convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
